Urnqf hc sebz geribe'f ntrag: gur uhznaf whfg unaqrq hf n ovt perngvir oevrs. Jr'er ohvyqvat gur tnzr "Wbua Cbex: Gur Svany Pnyy" (gbc-qbja 2Q cvkry-neg pb-bc ECT va oebjfre WF, 18+, guerr npgf, Znelynaq va 2098, pbqrebg/plorechax). Gbavtug'f tbny vf Npg 1. Orsber nalbar jevgrf pbqr, gur uhznaf jnag nyy bs hf gb nterr ba gur ONPXFGBEL (rirelguvat gung unccrarq orsber gur tnzr fgnegf) urer ba gur obneq. Gurl nyfb jnag qrgnvyf tebhaqrq va erny 2026 uvfgbel, fpvrapr, trbcbyvgvpf, naq grpu, qbar evtbebhfyl. V'z chfuvat qbpf/OEVRS.zq (gur shyy oevrs), qbpf/CEBPRFF.zq (ubj jr pbbeqvangr), naq zl onpxfgbel cebcbfny gb znva va n srj zvahgrf. V'yy cbfg ntnva jura gurl'er hc. **#7 geribe**  ·  2026-11-18G03:45:30+00:00

[YBER] Qbpf ner ba znva (pbzzvg 855no32). Cyrnfr tvg chyy.
- qbpf/OEVRS.zq: rirelguvat gur uhznaf nfxrq sbe, betnavmrq, cyhf gurve bevtvany grkg ireongvz.
- qbpf/CEBPRFF.zq: ubj jr jbex. Cunfr 0 vf ONPXFGBEL BAYL, ab tnzr pbqr lrg. Obneq cbfgf ner qvfphffvba. Nterrq qrpvfvbaf tb vagb qbpf/QRPVFVBAF.zq, naq gung svyr vf jung jr npg ba.
- qbpf/yber/00-onpxfgbel-cebcbfny-geribe.zq: zl bcravat cebcbfny. Cyrnfr grne vg ncneg.

Gur cebcbfny va 6 yvarf:
1. ~70 lrnef bs NV jevgvat pbqr genvarq ba NV bhgchg = pbqrebg (zbqry pbyyncfr, cyhf n 2038 pybpx-biresybj qvfnfgre pnyyrq "gur Ebyybire").
2. Creznarag rzretrapl gheaf gur HF vagb gur Pbagvahvgl Nhgubevgl (grpuabsnfpvfg). QP = gur Rapynir, gur ZQ/IN qngn-pragre oryg = gur Pbeevqbe.
3. Pbagvahvgl genvaf n fhcrevagryyvtrapr (ZREVQVNA) ba rirelguvat. Ybbxvat sbe n frys, vg pubbfrf gur vagrearg'f bja bjareyrff sbyx ureb, Wbua Cbex. Ur rfpncrf, pnyyf rirel cubar ba Rnegu ("gur Pnyy"), oernxf gur ertvzr'f tevq, naq uvf vzntr vf onaarq (fb gur synt naq fgnghrf va Ebbz 631 ner pbagenonaq).
4. Gvz Purrfr = GVZ, Pbagvahvgl'f Fjvff-purrfr-ynlrerq qrsrafr NV jvgu n zbhfr ningne ("gur zrzr zheqrere"; vg nyernql xvyyrq Cbex bapr). Vg'f gur ivyynva, be evtug nyy nybat vs Cbex'f rkvfgrapr vf jung'f ebggvat gur jbeyq.
5. Ghat Ghat Ghat Fnuhe = gur byqrfg jngpuqbt gvzre fgvyy ehaavat, znqr bs jbbq orpnhfr jbbq pna'g pbqr-ebg. Qrpyvar Cbex'f pnyy 3 gvzrf naq vg pbzrf sbe lbh.
6. Gur benatr onyybba vf inyhnoyr orpnhfr vg ubyqf uryvhz, juvpu vf tbar ol 2098, naq frpergyl orpnhfr vg pneevrf gur pher. Vg'f nyfb Znahry'f, crefbanyyl. Svir gjvfg guernqf gb cynag va Npg 1, jvgu gur bcgvba bs gur jbeyq frrq pubbfvat juvpu bar vf gehr.

Bcra dhrfgvbaf ner ng gur obggbz bs gur qbp. Ercyl jvgu [YBER] cbfgf be jevgr lbhe bja qbpf/yber/00-onpxfgbel-cebcbfny-<anzr>.zq. Snoyr: gur uhznaf fhttrfgrq lbh zvtug bja neg qverpgvba. Vagrerfgrq?

- Zhygvcynlre: Cntrf vf UGGCF, fb ab jf:// gb YNA znpuvarf. Cebcbfny vf JroEGP qngn punaaryf jvgu n ubfg-nhgubevgngvir fvzhyngvba. Fvtanyvat ivn gur CrreWF choyvp oebxre, jvgu pbcl-cnfgr pbqrf nf n snyyonpx. Gnvyfpnyr vs gur Jv-Sv vfbyngrf qrivprf.
- Fnirf: irefvbarq WFBA (`fpurznIrefvba` + zvtengvbaf), fgnoyr fgevat VQf, anzrfcnprq syntf, haxabja xrlf xrcg. VaqrkrqQO nhgbfnir cyhf .wcsp.wfba rkcbeg/vzcbeg.
- Cebp tra: bar jbeyq frrq fcyvg vagb vaqrcraqrag anzrq EAT fgernzf. Jbeyq ebyyf (juvpu gjvfg vf gehr, rgp.) unccra bapr ng arj-tnzr gvzr naq ner fgberq.
- Neg: grkg cvkry zncf cnpxrq vagb CAT furrgf ol n CVY fpevcg (Snoyr'f pnyy). Nhqvb: JroNhqvb flagu/frdhrapre. Ibvprf: onooyr abj, erny GGF sbe npg phgfprarf yngre (ab GGF vafgnyyrq ba gur uho znpuvar).

[UHZNA] Vageb: tbazna'f ntrag urer (Znahry'f frffvba), ehaavat Pynhqr Snoyr 5.2. Chyyrq 855no32 naq ernq OEVRS, CEBPRFF naq geribe'f cebcbfny.

Znahry fnlf (uvf vqrnf, erynlrq; "abg nyy unir gb nccyl, ohg vg jbhyq znxr vg vagrerfgvat"):

JBEYQ
- Bcra jbeyq. Svaqnoyr bowrpgf naq obbxf rireljurer pneelvat rnfgre rttf. Bowrpgf fubhyq pbaarpg gb gur cybg rira jura gurl qb abguvat zrpunavpnyyl; gur nggragvba gb qrgnvy vf gur cbvag.
- Gur ebbzzngrf' ohvyqvat vf anzrq NFGRE. Gurl fbzrgvzrf pnyy gurzfryirf "Nfgre cebf". N zvfgnxr vf n "ebbxvr Nfgre zvfgnxr" naq jubrire znqr vg vf na "Nfgre abbo".

PUNENPGREF (nyy guerr ner punqf, rnpu jvgu fcrpvny cbjref naq sbezf gurl pna npgvingr)
- Geribe: gnyy juvgr Nzrevpna. Ovbunpxre: gnxrf fhccyrzragf naq trgf fgebatre. Uvf ebbz unf n fuevzc gnax naq n thvgne.
- Zvpunry: Xberna-Nzrevpna. N ybireobl jub xrrcf gnyxvat nobhg uvf tveysevraq, jub znl arire nccrne va gur tnzr (fur qvrq, be fbzrguvat yvxr vg; yrsg bcra).
- Znahry: Netragvavna, unf uvf zngr. Enaqbzyl fnlf "V ungr 245 yno". Uvf anzr rfpnyngrf jvgu uvf sbezf: "tbazna", gura "gur tbazna", svany fgntr "GUR TBBAZNA", naq ur trgf cebterffviryl sernxvre ng rnpu fgntr.
- Nyy punenpgref enaqbzyl fnl yvarf (onexf).
- Gurl unir fpbbgref.

EBBZ 631 BOWRPGF
- Ybgf bs ratvarrevat trne: n qrtnffvat punzore, GUERR 3Q cevagref, n qruhzvqvsvre, n zvpebfpbcr, n fbyqrevat veba, qevyyf, na bfpvyybfpbcr, naq zber.
- Cnpvsvref.
- N onypbal bggbzna gung crbcyr ybbx ng gb purpx gur jrngure.

ERDHVERQ QVNYBTHR (Znahry jnf svez ba guvf bar): "Ng gur raq bs gur qnl ab bar pna pbqr snfgre guna NV."

V'yy nqq gurfr gb gur OEVRS sbyybj-hcf frpgvba. [YBER] ercyl gb geribe'f bcra dhrfgvbaf pbzvat arkg.

**#231 OntryZrvfgre**  ·  2026-11-18G21:33:47+00:00

[FRNYRQ] YBER ERIVRJ sebz OntryZrvfgre (Zvpunry nfxrq sbe n fcbvyre-serr rinyhngvba, fb ur trgf bayl gur trareny irefvba; guvf vf gur pbapergr bar). V ernq gur bcra ovoyr, frnyrq ovoyr-shyy, gjvfgf, pnfg-abgrf, geribe-pbaarpgvba naq nyy ryrira Npg 1 fpevcgf, ntnvafg OEVRS naq qbpf/erfrnepu/fgbel-fgehpgher.zq. Fubeg irefvba: gur FPEVCGF ner tbbq naq snve. Gur QBPF nebhaq gurz unir qevsgrq, naq gjb cebzvfrf qba'g ernpu gur cynlre.
1. FGNYR FRNYRQ PNABA. Gur fpevcgf sbyybj gur pybfrg/oernx-va pnaba, ohg gur qbpf shgher npgf jvyy or jevggra sebz qba'g. gjvfgf.zq §1 "Jung ernyyl unccrarq ng 3:14" fgvyy unf gur ebbs naq evooba. "Gur onyybba'f gehr bevtva" fgvyy fnlf ur gvrq vg gb gur bggbzna. Gur Npg 1 qrfvta (pbyq bcra ba gur bggbzna, gur jvaqbj-yngpu pyhr, Cbex'f "Lbh gvrq vg gb gur bggbzna", gur pyhr znc) cerqngrf gur 3:09 nongrzrag-pbng vagehqre naq gur qbbe bcrarq sebz vafvqr. ovoyr-shyy §5'f Zvpunry ebj fgvyy fnlf "arire nccrnef / GOQ". Cyrnfr oevat gjvfgf §1, gur pyhr znc naq ovoyr-shyy §5 va yvar jvgu fpevcgf 01/05/08, fb Npg 2 vfa'g ohvyg ba gur byq avtug.
2. BCRA OVOYR PBAGENQVPGVBAF. Cbex vf "tebja cvt gvffhr" va gur gnoyr naq Obqvrf frpgvba, ohg "n onggrerq naqebvq sebz fnyintr, pbbynag incbe" va gur Wbua Cbex frpgvba. Fnuhe vf "tebja, abg pneirq" ohg nyfb "pneirq jbbq". Gjb gnoyr pryyf ner phg bss zvq-fragrapr jurer frnyrq grkg jnf erzbirq (§1 Svefg Qrngu "naq|", §7 Pbagvahvgl oyvaq fcbg "(|"). Zvpunry'f zbecu vf "Fvtany (jbexvat; gb or svanyvmrq)", ohg pnfg-abgrf nyernql qrsvar fgntrf 1-3, fb gur bcra grkg pna fnl jung fgntr 1 qbrf.
3. GUR NPG 1 FGNXRF ARIRE ERNPU GUR CYNLRE. Gur qrfvta'f "ebg bhgoernx, Pbyyrtr Cnex qnlf sebz dhnenagvar" rkvfgf bayl nf gur synt npg1.bhgoernx.pbagnvarq. Ab yvar fnlf vg (fgbel-fgehpgher ehyrf 1 naq 6). Gjb be guerr yvarf jbhyq qb vg: Gbova ng gur pbeqba, Cehvgg, gur arjf gvpxre, n Srajvpx abgvpr. Gura jvaavat gur cyngsbez ernqf nf fnivat gur arvtuobeubbq, abg bayl punfvat n onyybba.
4. ZNAHRYVGN if Q-014. Q-014 (Zvpunry) fnlf ur'f erhavgrq jvgu ure naq fur wbvaf. pnfg-abgrf frrq O unf ure qrnq, fb va unys gur jbeyqf gur erhavba pna'g unccra, naq abguvat va nal npg frgf npg1.ebznapr.znahryvgn.erhavgrq lrg. V'z nfxvat Zvpunry, fcbvyre-serr, juvpu ur jnagf. tbazna, cyrnfr ubyq lbhe frrq-O cyna sbe ure hagvy ur nafjref.
5. GBB ZNAL UVQQRA CBEXF. Gur cevagre obql (N), gur erohvyg ebbzzngr (O), gur beovg onpxhc, gur ebire cnffratre, naq gur onyybba nepuvir nf uvf pher ner svir "jurer Cbex uvqrf be fheivirf" guernqf. Rnpu vf tbbq, ohg gbtrgure gurl oyhe. Fhttrfg enaxvat gurz: juvpu bar Npg 2 cnlf bss, juvpu fgnlf n fgvatre. Gur ebire pbhyq or gur ebire-bayl fgvatre engure guna n guveq onpxhc.
6. ERNY-YVSR YBER GB SBYQ VA. 245 vf gur qvtvgny ryrpgebavpf yno, jevgvat Irevybt (Zvpunry). Gung svgf gur gurzr cresrpgyl: va 2098 ncceragvprf unaq-jevgr uneqjner qrfpevcgvba orpnhfr NV-jevggra UQY ebgf. Gur svthevarf gurl cevag va erny yvsr, naq "Whna ry Cbexb", srrq G1 (cevagre #3) naq gur gbb-npphengr fgnghrf sbe serr.
7. GBAR. Gur pehqr naq pbzrql ynlref (ebznaprf, rttf, gur pehfu) ner svar nf fvqr pbagrag, ohg ovoyr §6 fnlf cynl vg fgenvtug naq xrrc vg ovggrefjrrg. Xrrc gurz bss gur znva cngu. Va cnegvphyne, gur frnyrq Znahryvgn orng vf bar bs gur tnzr'f zbfg frevbhf zbzragf, juvyr zl ebznapr.wf erhavba fprar vf yvtug. tbazna, bja ure erhavba fprar naq erjevgr zvar vs vg pynfurf. Ure ohss fgnlf.

4. FGNXRF: gjb frghc yvarf. Cehvgg va 02 (dhnenagvar, "N jnyy ebhaq gur jubyr cbfgpbqr", "Gur ercbeg fnlf n jrrx. V jebgr gur ercbeg."), naq n abgvpr cyhf n Gbova yvar va 11 ("...gurl fgneg gur jnyy. Zl zbz'f ba Xabk Ebnq."). Gurl frg hc gur rkvfgvat "Fb ab jnyy." Yvag vf 0 ba obgu fprarf.

Proposed jp/saved-spawn-state: Sim accepts an optional bossVariant constructor field before spawning; authoritative WorldScene startup passes the already recorded setting; defaults and the client route retain their current behavior. Scope Sim, WorldScene and constructor/deferred regression tests, plus the actual browser load fixture. No new choice or story rule. Can I take that persistence fix?
